While distributing books to the local Indians and Chinese, we are often hit with a question, “Is the author of this book alive?” And when we reply that Your Divine Grace has attained *samādhi,* some of the common replies are “This man is great,” “He is powerful,” “He is not scared,” “He is brave and straightforward,” “When we read the books we feel he is still alive.” And some even reply that they feel the presence of Your Divine Grace in the books. They take you as very special. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your glories are to be known by all and not limited to just a few. Anyone who reads your books automatically becomes attached to Your Divine Grace.

Amidst so many difficulties and so much confusion, we need all the strength and mercy of Your Divine Grace. It seems that we can appreciate Your Divine Grace only in your absence.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you placed your lotus feet in Malaysia in the year 1971, on the way to Australia. Many of us here in Malaysia regret that we were not able to serve Your Divine Grace in person at that time. Now we are serving you in separation. Still, we are getting the mercy from Your Divine Grace. We are not left out. Your mercy is for all.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, one area where we lack is preaching to the local Muslims, who form the majority of the population. It is our desire that at one of your future Vyāsa-pūjās will offer you some Muslim devotees. The local Muslims in Malaysia are called Malays, and their special advantage that their language makes regular use of words like “Hari” and “Ram.” And so it seems that they are getting the benefits of chanting the holy name unknowingly. Still, we desire that Your Divine Grace be introduced in their lives so they can get the full benefits in chanting.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we understand that on your visit to Malaysia you desired to see a temple built for Lord Kṛṣṇa in Malaysia. The devotees here are making a humble attempt to offer a nice temple to Lord Kṛṣṇa, and in this endeavor they depend on Your Divine Grace’s mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you also desired that the ISKCON congregation should work to support the local temples. In Malaysia we are working to this plan of yours. Now about fourteen main centers are being managed by the congregation here. And many *nāma-haṭṭa* programs around the country are run by congregation members.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as the years go by we see no end to apparently stalwart devotees falling victim to Māyā’s influence. We devotees of ISKCON Malaysia pray that you will always protect us neophytes from all the influences of this Age of Kali and keep us at your divine lotus feet. We know only that your lotus feet will help us cross over this material world and get us back to Godhead.

We pray to always be the servant of the servant at your divine lotus feet.

Your devotees in Malaysia.  
(compiled by Siṁheśvara dāsa)  
Regional Secretary ISKCON Malaysia

## Manchester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine mission.

Thank you for allowing us to remain yet another year in your service. Surely a fine example of causeless mercy, as we are hardly achieving anything; perhaps we are even impeding the preaching by our material desires. However, your own brilliant example and lucid teachings cannot easily be obscured, even by such fallen souls as us, and thus we are seeing people coming to your shelter, slowly but surely.

Indeed, it is your example that keeps us striving to spread your message, the message of Vāsudeva. Sometimes it is difficult. So few people seem interested, most are completely unable to understand even the most basic points, and many are antagonistic. It can be very frustrating seeing the world going to hell, oblivious of the fact that real solutions are on offer in your books. At present we have no platform to present those solutions, being for the most part marginalized as a minority religious group with some outdated beliefs that have little practical value. Your desire that Kṛṣṇa become as famous in Manchester as Manchester cotton was in India is still a far-off goal.

That is where your personal example provides endless inspiration. You too faced apathy and even antagonism, even in India. Your realizations about the effectiveness of Kṛṣṇa consciousness as a panacea for the world’s problems is obviously far greater than ours, so how much greater must your frustration have been? Yet you were never daunted in your efforts to preach. Without doubt, if we had faced anything like the tribulations you endured, we would have long ago given up.

On my wall I have a drawing of you ascending the gangway onto the *Jaladuta*. It is my favorite picture and it never fails to give me inspiration. Who could have guessed what a significant moment that was in the history of the world? Surely the demigods were assembled above that old ship as you made your way aboard. But only one man was there to see you off, a Mr. Bhattacharya. Here are his own words:

He was alone. A lone fighter. When he left, there was no one on the shore to bid him good-bye. No friends, no supporter, no disciple, nobody. Even if you call me, I was not a disciple of his. I was a disciple of somebody else. So I was not his follower. But due to shared love, I had very much respect for him. So, I was the only person standing on the shore to say him good-bye. No one was with me. I could not know that it was such an important thing.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, if I could ever go back in time to any particular moment, I think that would be the one. I would want to be there, cheering you on, relishing the incredible spirit and compassion you displayed. Even now, meditating on that moment, I find my desire to be your follower, and especially to spread your messages, is fueled more and more.

And when you reached America, what a daunting prospect that must have been! A cold, harsh, heartless land. What hope was there that you might succeed? In “Mārkine Bhāgavata Dharma”—another wonderful inspiration—you said,

My dear Lord Kṛṣṇa, You are so kind upon this useless soul, but I do not know why You have brought me here. Now you can do whatever you like with me. I guess You must have some business here; otherwise why would you bring me to this terrible place?...I do not know how the population will be able to understand the transcendental message of Vasudeva....How will they understand the mellows of devotional service?

Obviously you felt helpless. As Satsvarūpa Goswami nicely put it, describing your first journey into an American city, “Meat-eating, illicit sex, intoxication, and gambling—the very sins \[you\] had come to preach against—were proudly glamorized on mile after mile of billboards.” As he goes on to describe, you had come to teach the very opposite, that happiness is found not in sense gratification but in surrender to Kṛṣṇa. What a massive task!

Of course, the rest is history. You succeeded wonderfully, and we are here now as the evidence of that. But out there in the world things have still not changed very much. The same billboards, the same sins, the majority of the population still fully absorbed in degrading themselves to the lowest possible status, quite unable to understand the message of Vāsudeva. So we are battling on, dreaming of achieving the kind of success that we know will only come when we reach the same kind of surrender shown by you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything: The complete science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the perfect example of how to live it, and the perfect example of preaching. Now it is up to us. We pray that you allow us to carry on trying to give up our useless material desires and thereby become useful. Meanwhile it seems that you are still our main preacher, and probably it will always be that way. That is quite proper. Surely we can hope for little more than to be one day recognized as having rendered you some valuable service and thus expanded your glories—*ebe yaśa ghuṣuk tribhuvana*.

With repeated obeisances at your feet, we remain  
Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Manchester, UK.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa Dharma dāsa)

## Manila

**Your Unlimited Glories**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our most humble obeisances at your most glorious lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how and where can we begin to glorify your unlimited glories? There are no limits to your sublime personality, and what to speak of your transcendental pastimes and achievements? However, just as in the *Bhagavad-gītā* Arjuna was able to see the unlimited nature of Śrī Kṛṣṇa because the Lord revealed it to Him, so we can understand something of your transcendental attributes because by your mercy you have kindly allowed us to see, opening our eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. Of course, we are unfit to glorify you because we have led very inglorious lives. But just as the grandfather derives pleasure from the broken language of his small grandchildren, similarly, we pray that you will become pleased with our humble attempt to glorify you.

Of all the glorious attributes you possess, one stands out as unique and most important. And that is your quality of acting in a perfectly exemplary manner, showing the true meaning of *ācārya*—one who teaches by example. The variety of your exemplary activities is inconceivable and extraordinary, truly spiritually surcharged and transcendental. No one can equal or surpass you on this basic point alone. Your purity is so strong because whatever you spoke and wrote in your books was manifested in your sublime personality. In essence, you did not only speak the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness but also lived it day by day. Teaching by example, especially in Kali-yuga (the age of quarrel and hypocrisy), in both the academic and spiritual circles, is almost unheard of. That is why you had so much power in preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Of course, the evidence of your preaching potency was the sudden increase (continuing to this day) of disciples and temples around the world. You alone had the will power and potency to spread the teachings of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. The holy names resounded throughout the world and became popular because you began it all with your humble chanting at Tompkins Square Park. And *prasādam* was relished by one and all when you introduced the Sunday Love Feast. Today, *prasādam* is readily available not only at the Love Feast in all your temples but also through your Food for Life program and various ISKCON restaurants.

Again, because of your purity and honesty in word and deed, you not only made the holy names and *prasādam* widely available but soundly defeated bogus philosophies and big scholarly scientists. You made no compromise with material so-called scientists, unafraid and unhindered in declaring Kṛṣṇa consciousness the supreme science.

And so, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your glories shall continue on and on—without limit. It has been more than twenty years since you left this material world, but each day more and more fallen conditioned souls are reclaimed. Therefore, there is no end to your glories, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You have said that ISKCON is your body. So as long as there is at least one ISKCON devotee left standing, your glories shall continue.

On this most glorious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the devotees of the Manila *yātrā* pray that glorification of your unlimited glories may go on increasing, never to be stopped under any circumstance. We also pray that you will kindly bestow upon us your mercy, which is as unlimited as your other glories are. We are simply your fallen grandchildren, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and one drop of your mercy will certainly quench the thirst of our struggling creeper of devotional service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!!

Your aspiring grandchildren in Manila, Philippines.  
(written by Śrīdāmā dāsa)

## Manipur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Most worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We are the unworthy disciples of His Holiness Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami Mahārāja, who is one of your most trusted and beloved disciples. Prior to our meeting with our Guru Mahārāja, we were completely ignorant about our life’s mission. Whatever we did was only for the gratification of our senses. We could think and act only in terms of our body, community, nationality, kith and kin, etc., without cultivating real spiritual knowledge *(Bhāgavata* culture*).* Now with his unlimited divine blessings and instructions we have been made fully aware of the obligations we are expected to meet toward the fulfillment of the desires of Your Divine Grace.

By your unbounded mercy, you have bestowed upon us a precious divine gift in the person of Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Mahārāja as your representative to the devotees of ISKCON Manipur. Now we can understand the meaning of a most important instruction Your Divine Grace imparted to your disciple Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Mahārāja: Living in this miserable material world, we should “make the best use of a bad bargain” and try to become Kṛṣṇa conscious.

The Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa is really great and is the ocean of mercy. By His sweet will He sent Your Divine Grace to this mortal world to save the people who are drowning in the whirling waves of this Kali-yuga. Without your unlimited divine blessings, we are like fish out of water.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, to us you are a true representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You imparted Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all the fallen souls of this earthly planet. You are the incarnation of Devarṣi Nārada Muni. Kindly shower your unlimited blessings upon us so that we can eternally remain engaged in your eternal devotional service.

Your most fallen servants,  
The devotees in Manipur, India.

## Markham

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you are the most perfect spiritual guide?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How your teachings and the original *Vedas* do coincide?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How before we met you we were mystified?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you saved us from *māyā’s* strong tide?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you showed us that other “gurus” lied?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you proved that you are so purified?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness far and wide?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you made our material thoughts subside?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you told us to preach and remain unified?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa you so glorified?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you saved so many lives?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you taught us instructions by which we should abide?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you showed us to shed our foolish pride?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How you showed us the path to Kṛṣṇa’s side?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How in you we can fully confide?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How we beg that your mercy on us does not slide?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we describe  
How forever under your lotus feet we wish to hide?

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
*Jaya* Gurudeva!

Your humble servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara Mandir in Markham, Ontario, Canada.  
(written by Mīnākṣī-devī dāsī)

## Melbourne

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

In some religious traditions the followers conceive of God as father. However, you have taught us that with such a conception there is a danger. Since the father is the provider and the children are the recipients, one might see God as the order-supplier rather than as the worthy recipient of loving service.

When we consider you as our spiritual father, we cannot comprehend the vastness of our inheritance. You have given us Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy; Vedic knowledge and wisdom; *kīrtana; bhajan;* tasty *prasādam;* ISKCON, a vibrant institution of spiritual association; the Deities and Deity worship; the *guru-paramparā; Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam; Caitanya-caritāmṛta;* so many essential spiritual instructions; and, above all, the holy names, the only means of deliverance in this dark Age of Kali.

Forgive us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, if we sometimes resemble the sons and daughters of the wealthy who squander their inheritance, not understanding the struggle their forefathers underwent to establish their legacy. In this regard we are pleased to report that each year the Janmāṣṭamī, Vyāsa-pūjā, and other festivals are better attended, our *nāma-haṭṭa* groups are growing, congregational support and book distribution are increasing, and more *prasādam* is being distributed. Also, in the last year we have established a downtown “loft” preaching center based on the 26 Second Avenue preaching center you established.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us to never see you as an order-supplier; rather, let us increase our loving service to you through the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

Your humble servants at the Melbourne Mahāprabhu Mandir.  
(written by Yadu Śreṣṭha dāsa)

## Monterrey

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Very beloved spiritual master, please accept our humble obeisances.  
You are the only hope in this material world. The name  
Abhaya was given to you because you dissipate the fear of material life.  
So many souls are witnesses to this. Please allow us  
Always to take shelter at your feet by developing devotion to you.

**P**rabhupāda, you have the power to engage many devotees and  
Unify them in Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s devotional service. Therefore we exclaim  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Our only  
Ambition is to serve you eternally in your divine mission,

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we would like to humbly request of you  
1 thing: Please engage us in the service of Śrī Brajendra Kumāra, which is divided into  
9 processes, which begin with *sravaṇam,* or hearing. Please let us hear from you attentively, and let us progress gradually up to *sakhyam,* which is the eighth, and *ātma-nivedanam,* which is number  
9 and which consists of fully surrendering at the lotus feet of the Lord. Without the mercy of Your Divine Grace, that is not possible. These  
9 processes are the only way to engage the mind and senses in the service of Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda we would like to renew our promises to you this day—to chant sixteen rounds every day, to follow the four regulative principles, and to follow all other principles of *sādhana-bhakti,* such as attending *maṅgala-ārati,* Tulasī *ārati, sundara-ārati,* and classes on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā,* and also studying your books carefully and distributing them worldwide.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept this humble prayer at Your Divine Grace’s lotus feet on this holy day of your appearance. Even though we are not pure devotees, our only desire is to serve Your Divine Grace’s mission in this world.

Aspiring to be the servants of Your Divine Grace, we remain  
Your grand-disciples in Monterrey, Mexico.

## Montreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace and your faithful disciples, who are sincerely trying to carry out your sacred orders.

We first pray that Lord Śrī Caitanya give us the inspiration to glorify Your Divine Grace.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O great transcendental sage, you are the most merciful and exalted personality of all times. Your love for us is shining like the full moon in the black night of this Kali-yuga. We are the most fallen souls, but still, with patience and tolerance, you are showing us the way to go back home, back to Godhead, within this lifetime. It is the ultimate benediction of all time! With compassion, you are teaching the whole world that Śrī Kṛṣṇa is the eternal loving father of everyone, anxious to re-establish an intimate loving relationship with us.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have brought us direct association with the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, through the *mahā-mantra,* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. Now, anyone can take this Kṛṣṇa consciousness process and develop his dormant love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. By your causeless mercy, even those who are lower than *śūdras,* like us, can take to this scientific devotional process.

You have also brought, translated, and distributed the sacred Vedic literatures so that we can get full knowledge of the transcendental science of *bhakti-yoga*. And you are still acting as the *śikṣā-guru* through your books and other sources of transcendental instructions.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have accomplished so many miracles. Among all of them, the most astonishing one is to have turned fallen souls like ourselves into bona fide Vaiṣṇavas. By this miracle, even fallen souls like us can associate with saintly Vaiṣṇavas and become Kṛṣṇa conscious by the power of their blessings. This *sādhu-saṅga,* as mentioned by Lord Caitanya, is very rare in this age of hypocrisy and very hard to maintain also. For neophytes like us to become faithful and fixed in our devotional service, strong *sādhu-saṅga* is essential. Through this *sādhu-saṅga* one gets a golden opportunity to make spiritual advancement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray at your divine lotus feet that you may kindly allow us to mature in our association with devotees within ISKCON and thus protect us from the strong attacks of *māyā* that we are experiencing right now.

In ISKCON Montreal we are trying to follow your various instructions, although we only imperfectly succeed. We are endeavoring to develop a nice family spirit among the devotees so that when we receive guests they can experience a peaceful and spiritual atmosphere, like that in Goloka Vṛndāvana. We are working very hard to increase our faith more and more in Śrī Guru and Śrī Gaurāṅga. We are trying to maintain a high standard of Deity worship of Their most attractive Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara. As often as possible we go on *harināma* and book distribution, although we don’t have a regular party of book distributors. We are expanding our boutique sales and planning to do some outside publicity. Also, we started a Food for Life program in march 1998, and it is still going out once a week, with an average of fifty regular recipients.

We would like to conclude this offering by mentioning our land project for ISKCON Montreal. We know from your books that application of *varṇāśrama-dharma* is very important to help people cultivate Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You have mentioned several times that unless a society follows *varṇāśrama-dharma* it is not considered human or civilized. And real application of *varṇāśrama-dharma* can best be done on a farm community. So in the near future ISKCON Montreal plans to have its own farm community, where various facilities and Vedic sciences will be developed.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will never be able to repay what you have done and are still doing for us. Our only desire is to always be engaged in the service of your sincere servants.

Your servants at ISKCON Montreal, Quebec, Canada.  
(written by Kānāi Kṛṣṇa dāsa)

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Recently, by Lord Kṛṣṇa’s causeless mercy, we have been given the most wonderful opportunity of serving the community of Vaiṣṇavas in your preaching mission in a very insignificant way. And the Vaiṣṇavas, merciful and kind as they are, are inspiring us with their encouraging words and heartfelt blessings. This is just the result of trying to become the servant of your servants. Śrīla Prabhupāda, truly, the easiest way to attain your mercy and the mercy of Kṛṣṇa is to please the Vaiṣṇavas, which is the simplest yet the most challenging task at the same time. We beg at your feet that we may always focus our attention on the three most important principles that sustain us and help us advance properly:

*Nāma-ruci*—a taste for the holy name.  
*Jīva-dayā*—compassion for the conditioned souls.  
*Vaiṣṇava-sevā—*service to the Vaiṣṇavas.

We have no power to do one properly without doing the other two, because each sustains the other two.

We beg always to remain the servants of your servants.

Your devotees at the Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir in Chowpatty, Mumbai, India.

## Mumbai (Juhu)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Lord Caitanya’s movement has spread far and wide by your desire. We pray on this most holiest of days to develop a mood to assist you in your preaching. On our own we have no *śakti,* or power. Our ability to preach comes from a desire to cooperate with one another to spread the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Any other desire we have only becomes an impediment to your preaching.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has mentioned in His *Śikṣāṣṭaka* that He does not desire wealth, beautiful women, or any number of followers. We pray on this day that the desire for these things be driven from our hearts.

On this auspicious day we rededicate our lives to helping you serve Lord Kṛṣṇa. To approach the Supreme Lord directly is very difficult, but if we come to you first and surrender ourselves, then and only then is it possible to please the Supreme Lord.

So, although we have many faults and are not very serious we humbly beg your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg for your mercy so we may be empowered to:

1\. Give up our material desires.

2\. Deliver the fallen conditioned souls by distributing your transcendental books.

3\. Distribute huge quantities of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*

4\. Spread the holy name by performing *harināma-saṅkīrtana* all over Mumbai.

5\. Worship Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārī, Gaura-Nitāi, and Sītā-Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, and Hanumān with proper care and attention.

6\. Have all the devotees in your Juhu Mumbai temple properly situated in devotional service according to *daivi-varṇāśrama*.

7\. Use every square foot of Hare Krishna Land for spreading the mission of Lord Caitanya.

8\. Work together for your pleasure.

We, the members of ISKCON Juhu, ask the above benedictions. Surely if you are kind upon us we can do something wonderful. Otherwise, we will only fight with one another and become useless.

Please save us from such a situation by you merciful glance.

Your servants at ISKCON Juhu, Mumbai, India.  
(written by Bhīma dāsa)

## Mumbai (Mira Road)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

The devotees here are so much enlivened by your presence in your *mūrti* form. You sit very humbly, accepting our daily offerings of prayers and obeisances. It is only your mercy that allows to survive, and even thrive, in this dark age of Kali-yuga.

To remember, glorify, worship and serve such a pure devotee as you is the perfection of one’s life.

By your causeless mercy, we’ve gotten the opportunity to be a small part of your great spiritual family. You’ve really built a big house in which the whole world can live. Although we have no opportunity to personally assist you, nonetheless we are associating with your through your disciples and books. We all can see how people’s lives change when they read your books. In this way they become fit to go back home, back to Godhead.

It is amazing to see how our own lives have changed, turning into that of real human beings. But the most wonderful thing we can be a part of is Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* mission. And out of your and your disciples causeless mercy we can give real hope to the people in general so that they will become successful in their lives.

Please consider our pitiable condition and shower your mercy on us so that we can become pure instruments you may use in carrying on this *saṅkīrtana* mission. We offer our obeisances again and again at our lotus feet.

Your humble servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārī Temple, Mira Road, Mumbai, India.

## Munich

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Caitanya Mahāprabhu predicted that the holy name would be chanted in every town and village. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have dedicated your life to being the Lord’s instrument that would spread the seeds of devotion all over the world.

Because you had so much faith in the instruction of your spiritual master, you have been successful in this divine mission. You have inspired so many people to worship Kṛṣṇa and to spread His glories further.

On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we want to express our gratitude for everything you’ve done for us. We are happy to report that some of your transcendental seeds are also sprouting in Munich.

Please help us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to be good gardeners who protect the tender *bhakti* plants and nicely care for them, especially when the surroundings are rough for them to grow properly.

We are quite helpless. We might endeavor, but our plans are fallible. Kṛṣṇa is the ability in man. Therefore we need your help, so that Kṛṣṇa’s plan may manifest in our hearts and we can act properly to satisfy you and our Lord.

Your servants in Munich, Germany.

## Nagpur

Dearest beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

A lot of changes have taken place here in Nagpur over the past year. We have moved to a new, more prominent location in a better part of the city, Abhayankar Nagar, and a built a large new temple room for our growing congregation. It is on the main road, which gets us noticed. Every day we receive regular visitors, who stop to take *darśana* of the Deities, to chant *japa,* or purchase one of your books and ask questions to the resident devotees. In the evening the temple room is filled with guests loudly and enthusiastically chanting the holy name. Hundreds of people drive, cycle, or walk past the temple and offer respect to Their beautiful Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha and Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauracandra as they pass by.

We must confess, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that even though ISKCON has been in Nagpur for so many years, we have been very slow to push on the preaching. However, over the past year we have had a big boost to the preaching activities and have come a long way. This is due to the input of your beloved disciple His Holiness Lokanāth Swami Mahārāja, who has been mercifully visiting us regularly to enthuse and guide us with your divine teachings. And he wishes to spend even more time here. The most important improvement is the growing number of congregational members, many of whom are seriously taking to the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and becoming more active. So many people’s lives are changing because of the mercy you have showed them.

Last Kārttika we took a group of fifty local congregational and life members to Vṛndāvana to visit the holy pastime places of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, which you so kindly revealed to us. Kṛṣṇa consciousness is expanding more and more among young people. Nagpur is a college and university city, so we are attracting many young people from the student community. Some of them have been regularly taking part in temple activities for a few years now. This year we started The ISKCON Youth Forum and a Sunday school, which present Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the younger generation in a way that appeals to them.

The devotees are expanding book distribution with great enthusiasm, and a new temple shop has been built, which displays your books to the visitors and passersby. We have also improved the standard of Deity worship for the pleasure of Their lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha and Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauracandra, and this higher standard is reflected in al the other temple activities. We are fortunate to be close to the appearance place of Śrīmatī Rukmiṇī-devī, the goddess of fortune herself. Locally she is known as Vidarbhī. This region of Maharashtra is still called Vidarbha now, as it was five thousand years ago. So we are blessed with her remembrance.

A beautiful ISKCON lotus is blooming in Nagpur, a small offering to your lotus feet.

Please continue to shower your merciful glances upon us and give us the strength to continue spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Nagpur.

Your spiritual grandchildren at ISKCON Nagpur, India.  
(written by Anurāga dāsa)

## Nava-Jiyaḍa-Nṛsiṁha-Kṣetra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We are pleased to offer our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet on this glorious day, as on every day of our lives, made glorious by service to you. We pray that you will always accept our obeisances, even if sometimes we are wanting in the attentiveness and humility appropriate to this gesture.

In the *prema-dhvani* with which you showed us to conclude *kīrtanas,* you always ended with “All glories to the assembled devotees! All glories to the assembled devotees! All glories to the assembled devotees!” Each time the devotees responded with “Hare Kṛṣṇa,” as the only proper response to this triple blessing by Your Divine Grace. We know that you genuinely desired that your followers be blessed with all spiritual glories: As you have explained, a Vedic statement repeated three times indicates very strong emphasis; also, you took every opportunity to recognize progress in your budding sprouts of *bhakti;* thirdly, you insisted that we imbibe the lesson that devotees—all who assemble to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and hear from your lotus lips—are indeed glorious in the eyes of Kṛṣṇa.

As time passes, and the configurations of assembled devotees in your movement shift—by expansion, reduction, aging, changes of *āśrama,* politics, policy changes, changes of location, or whatever—we want to remember the conviction with which you would chant “All glories to the assembled devotees! All glories to the assembled devotees! All glories to the assembled devotees!” and imbibe this spirit of conviction in our own assembly of devotees.

Here in Nṛsiṁha-Kṣetra, in the heart of Europe, Nṛsiṁha-deva blesses His devotees with His right hand while sheltering Prahlāda on his left thigh. Although few devotees reside here, we know that devotees throughout Europe and beyond are assembled, in their hearts, at Nṛsiṁha-deva’s lotus feet. They seek His assurances for protection from the dangers that, like so many demons, seem to mount opposition to your movement or seem to call attention away from the important business at hand. They seek also to expand their vision, to begin to see as you and Kṛṣṇa are teaching us to see—that every living entity is potentially a candidate for the Lord’s pure devotional service. They seek, even as visible numbers in temples may wax and wane, to see the devotees—whether momentarily present in the assembly or not—as truly glorious by virtue of their sincere desires—however covered they may sometimes be—to serve the servants of Kṛṣṇa.

As we ask ourselves what lessons Kṛṣṇa is trying to teach us by the trials and tribulations we individually and collectively experience in the course of practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we want to always remember the assuring voice of Your Divine Grace repeating “All glories to the assembled devotees! All glories to the assembled devotees! All glories to the assembled devotees!” knowing that your words, if we take them properly (with “Hare Kṛṣṇa!”) will indeed come true, and we will one day be able to comprehend this amazing truth and benediction: “All glories to the assembled devotees!”

Your servants at Nava-Jiyaḍa-Nṛsiṁha-Kṣetra, Germany.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-kṣetra dāsa)

## New Badarikāśrama

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace and to your most beloved appearance day! All glories to your eternal preaching!

Our offering is being written on the disappearance day of Vṛndāvana dāsa Ṭhākura, the Vyāsa of *caitanya-līlā*. As a pure representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, you delivered to us the treasure of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. And by the mercy of Śrīla Vṛndāvana dāsa Ṭhākura and Your Divine Grace, we received the jewel of *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* after the amazing marathon in New Dvārakā in 1975.

Śrīla Vṛndāvana dāsa Ṭhākura, the authorized writer of the pastimes of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, is as good as Śrīla Vyāsadeva. He has described the pastimes in such a way as to make them sweeter and sweeter. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, *Ādi-līlā* 13.48\]

Now you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have been empowered to present the pastimes and philosophy in an even sweeter way. Your books are captivating the hearts of Kali-yuga’s inmates.

What great good fortune we had here in Denver when you visited during the summer of 1975 and preached about the nectar of the Sixth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in your classes:

We are discussing the process of purification. Different methods have been described, by *prāyaścitta* and by *tapasya*. We have discussed. And then *kevalayā bhakta*. *Bhakti* includes everything—*karma, jñāna, yoga,* everything. And it is specially recommended that by austerities and other methods, there is possibility, but it may not be successful. But if we adopt this process, devotional service, then it is sure. So this purificatory process means *nivṛtti-mārga*. And *pravṛtti-mārga* means without any knowledge where we are going on, rushing on—we are doing everything, whatever we like—that is called *pravṛtti-mārga*....

But our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is teaching people that “Don’t live irresponsibly.”...To become a staunch devotee of Kṛṣṇa, perfect devotee of Kṛṣṇa, means one has become freed from all reaction of sinful life....Anyone who is engaged in Kṛṣṇa’s service with firm conviction and devotion, it is to be understood that he is now free from all reaction of sinful activities. A Vaiṣṇava never commits any sinful activities, and whatever he had done in the past, that is also finished....Or in other words, if you devoutly engage yourself in the service of the Lord, then certainly you are freed from all reaction of sinful activities. So how it is possible?...You have to take shelter of a person who is a devotee of Kṛṣṇa, and you have to serve. That means you have to accept a devotee, a true devotee, a pure devotee, as your guide. That is our process....By the grace of Kṛṣṇa there are pure devotees, so one has to take shelter of him.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us that we may fully take shelter of you and the Vaiṣṇavas. You are providing this shelter for all the world because you are the nonstop, empowered, pure representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva. Please may we also be empowered by you to distribute this message.

Thank you for coming to Denver in 1975 and for being here with us now every day. All glories to your mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Thank you for allowing us to participate and for the opportunity to help others also take part in the *saṅkīrtana* movement. We just want to please you.

Your servants at New Badarikāśrama, Denver, Colorado, USA.

## New Biharvan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement! And all glories to you, the glorious commander in His army of saṅkīrtanists!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have said that anything without Kṛṣṇa is *śūnya,* zero. But when we add Kṛṣṇa, who is the only 1, six zeroes become a million.

On this 1999 Vyāsa-pūjā day, the world is awash with many *śūnyas,* zeroes. We are at the onset of the new millennium. On one hand everyone is mesmerized by what the three new zeroes will bring. On the other hand many are frightened by the Y2K bug, a problem caused by two little *śūnyas*. The leaders of society are scampering to find a solution to the bug. Some are fixing computer programs, others are stocking up on food and water, and still others are moving into seclusion as they prepare for the world to collapse.

But they do not realize that only you possess the true antidote.

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In fact, it is your personal mission to free the world of these threatening mathematical and philosophical zeroes; you bring meaning to the *śūnyas* by adding the 1.

Right around the time you appeared on this planet, a big *śūnyavādī* from India extolled the glories of zero at the World Religions Conference in Chicago. But in the end, the impact of his *śūnya* talk was simply *śūnya*. He had forgotten the most important element—the 1 that turns zero into ten.

It is you who brought to us that missing 1. In the sixties, when the programmers were busy planting the Y2K zeroes, you were busy planting Ones. Through your Bhaktivedanta purports, thousands found the missing One—Kṛṣṇa, who is one without a second.

But billions more are still mired in endless zeroes, still ignorant of the most important One. If we could provide them with the One, through your books, *prasādam,* and temples, the world may not collapse after all.

To help with this effort, your humble servants at New Biharvan are attempting to build a new Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Bihārī temple as an offering to your lotus feet. By your presence, it will become a place where people can come to relieve themselves of the great burden of their zeroes and find respite in Kṛṣṇa’s holy name, your Bhaktivedanta purports, and *prasādam*. Please give us your mercy on this Vyāsa-pūjā day, so that we may be able to fight the onslaught of *śūnyas* and help others find the one—Kṛṣṇa.

Looking for shelter under your lotus feet,  
Your little bugs at New Biharvan, Boise, Idaho.  
(written by Anantarūpa dāsa)

## New Delhi (Noida)

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace, whose teachings are shining a light for us all to follow.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you said you wanted ten to twelve centers in and around Delhi. We are developing as one of those centers here in Noida. We are functioning as a fully-fledged ISKCON temple—one of *your* temples, Śrīla Prabhupāda, following the daily *sādhana* program you gave us, from *maṅgala-ārati* through to evening *Bhagavad-gītā* class. We worship small Deities of Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā and also small brass Gaura-Nitāi Deities. We hold weekly Saturday feast programs, which started out with only fifty or sixty people but now attracts as many as 250. They come to see what you are freely offering them. Then they are captivated by your formula of *kīrtana*, *kṛṣṇa-kathā,* and *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*.

Although this is our first offering from Noida, many of the devotees here have been practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness for several years. Previously they had to travel some distance to get to the temple and so were restricted in their attendance, but now many of them can come daily or at least more regularly to their own local center to cultivate their Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Many of them are enthusiastically and regularly hosting programs in their homes and taking full advantage of this new temple.

Their contributions, both financially and in the form of manpower, have enabled us to hold many functions and launch several programs. Over the past year and a half since the center started, Food for Life has been distributing hot *kicharī* and *halavah* weekly. Recently a devotee stepped forward and offered to regularly sponsor the purchase of grains, and this has allowed us to increase our Food for Life *prasādam* distribution to 1,500 plates a week.

Attendance at festivals is good, especially for Janmāṣṭamī, with more than 10,000 local people attending last year. And at our sixth annual *Gītā* Advent festival, 2,500 chanted, danced, and feasted with us. Next year we hope to hold our first Ratha-yātrā.

Our Noida center is turning out to be a steady center, with weekly *harināma,* book distribution, a developing congregation, and a strong desire to expand your mission. All these activities are going on by your mercy and for your pleasure. We also have a Nitya Seva program to enable the center to be supported by its congregation, and in return we try our best to facilitate their needs to grow in spiritual life.

Noida is a bit out of the way, but still many ISKCON devotees visit, and for the two million residents of Noida your new temple here is a great blessing, putting them in contact with you, your books, your *prasādam*, your devotees, and your mercy.

We are not big devotees or a big temple. We are just trying to do what we can, in whatever way we can. We are making mistakes and making changes, but all along the way we are trying to serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. For nearly all of us here you are our spiritual grandfather. It is said that the grandfather is more kind to the grandchild than the father. Please, we beg you to continue your kindness upon us. Please help us in our spiritual lives and teach us to help others in theirs. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us everything.

Your servants at the Noida temple in New Delhi, India.

## New Dvārakā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**The Perfect One**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You are the perfect pioneer,  
Perseverant, austere, and bold,  
Traversing hell without a fear,  
The story’s told.

You are the perfect teacher:  
For selfish gain you had no thought.  
Through acts of pure devotion  
We were taught.

You are the perfect mystic:  
Although you had no magic wand,  
You transformed lowly black crows  
Into swans

You are the perfect writer:  
Your treasured purports guide us through—  
Unending spiritual knowledge  
Left by you.

You are the perfect musician:  
From you flowed transcendental sounds;  
You spread the holy name through cities  
And towns.

You are the perfect artist,  
Painting pictures the soul can see,  
Giving vision to blinded eyes  
Of reality.

You are the perfect builder:  
A house you built for all to dwell,  
A Vaikuṇṭha oasis  
Here in hell.

You are the perfect gardener:  
The seeds of *bhakti* that you’ve sown  
Will blossom into creepers  
When they’re full-grown.

You are the perfect father:  
So merciful, yet firm and grave,  
You set us on the course back home,  
The path you paved.

You are the perfect disciple:  
Your guru’s words were life and soul;  
To take us back to Kṛṣṇa’s feet  
Your only goal.

You are the perfect well-wisher,  
Doing the highest welfare work,  
Saving suffering souls from repeated  
Rebirths.

You are the perfect physician,  
Curing us of lust’s disease,  
Prescribing the simple, healthy life,  
Sin-free.

You are the perfect alchemist:  
Your formula from days of old  
Is turning hardened leadlike hearts  
To purest gold.

You are the perfect captain,  
Guiding us through tempest waves;  
Your books we cast to drowning souls,  
Thus saved.

You are the perfect soldier:  
Like Arjuna you spurned defeat,  
Fighting for the Lord with each breath  
You breathed.

You are the perfect master:  
Nowhere in you could flaw be found;  
From chains of fruitive karma  
We were unbound.

You are the perfect servant,  
Ever dependent upon the Lord.  
A humble, pure devotee  
Rightly adored.

You are that one bright moon  
That shines ’midst countless stars,  
Bathing the world in light  
In its darkest hour.

Yes, you are everything to us.  
And that is why we’re here:  
To glorify you eternally,  
Our Prabhupāda dear.

Your ever-aspiring servants at New Dvārakā-dhāma, Los Angeles, California, USA.  
(written by Guṇāvatī-devī dāsī)

## New Ekacakra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisance unto the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

This is my first offering to your Vyāsa-pūjā book because till now I could not imagine that I would be worthy to write one. I thought I could only listen.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am very thankful to you for your books. When I saw your books I was very much attracted, even though I had never been very fond of books and the devotee selling them could not say much about the contents. When I read one of your small books, it was like a gulp of fresh air; I suddenly had a goal in my life, and you answered so many of my questions.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, from that time your instructions have helped me to live and not lose sight of the goal of life. My service is connected with teaching the devotees; I always try to help the devotees see the value of your books. I am happy when somebody is attracted to your books; this inspires me to continue my service. A Vaiṣṇava is a person who can inspire others to become Vaiṣṇavas by his own example.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, although I never met you, by association with your sincere disciples I have realized that you are a divine personality possessing all the wonderful qualities of a Vaiṣṇava, qualities that attract many people. Your sincere followers are able to overcome many difficulties to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for your compassion. You came to this world to spread the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and save us from materialistic life. A Vaiṣṇava is like a desire tree, and only by his causeless mercy can conditioned souls reach the perfection of human life—pure love of God.

The aspiring servants of your servants in New Ekacakra-dhāma, Ekaterinburg, Russia.  
(written by Taralākṣī-devī dāsī)

## New Goloka Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda!

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, and on behalf of the residents of New Goloka Dhāma and the congregation, we would like to rededicate ourselves to your transcendental preaching mission and focus on your eternal position in our lives. You are everyone’s *śikṣā-guru,* for by your instructions we are taught how to please Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. You have engaged us in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, and it is by your mercy that we are able to chant the *mahā-mantra* and engage in pure devotional service. You have demystified spiritual life and given us a tangible way to approach the Supreme Lord.

This is a difficult time for your ISKCON movement, but we see hope in the smiling innocent faces of your granddisciples who are coming to take shelter at your lotus feet. Our Society is still young, and we can learn from our past mistakes and push on your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement with renewed enthusiasm given to us by your books, lectures, and songs. Yet we know it must pain you to see these difficulties, and we pray to resolve these differences and to be united under your care and instructions.

Today we were reading your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 8.22.29–30. Bali Mahārāja surrenders everything to Lord Vāmanadeva. You explained that the Lord gave a huge test to His devotee, and simultaneously He also gave him the strength to tolerate such a seemingly adverse situation. Bali Mahārāja became glorious and renowned for his complete surrender, faithfulness, strength, and tolerance. In the same way, so many of your disciples and granddisciples have been faced with adversity and tests, but when we remain faithful and loyal to your instructions, there will be all success. This movement will grow strong and will spread love of Godhead all over the world, as predicted by you and the previous Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas*.

As we write this offering, the devotees are in the temple room chanting the *sundara-ārati* prayers to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda. You have given us this congregational chanting, which purifies our hearts and destroys all ignorance and doubt. Please continue to bless our community so that we can be an ideal example of Vaiṣṇava life and society. In the *Āyur-veda* we see that the life airs of the body must be in balance, and similarly the *āśramas* of our Society must be functioning properly and working together cooperatively for the good health of the whole Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Please help us to always see this broader view of your ISKCON so that we will cooperate and preach nicely together for your pleasure.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for saving us and giving us this wonderful life of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and for taking us back home, back to Godhead.

Sincerely,  
Your servants at New Goloka Dhāma, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-priyā-devī dāsī)

## New Govardhana (Australia)

One drop of the nectar of the mercy of Śrīla Prabhupāda can and will deliver every one of us. Please, therefore, above all else, search out this nectar and somehow taste it. It is the very life of pure devotional service and the essence of love of God.

**Deep in the Dust of Your Lotus Feet**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept my humble obeisances.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

I am nothing more than a tiny fool trying to gain the mercy of a Vaiṣṇava. I have habitually and consistently mistaken ignorance for truth. I have uselessly sought perfection in a place where there can be none, and all the instruments of my perception are imperfect. In this pitiable condition, where is there hope for one like me? Therefore I can only fall down, “deep in the dust of your lotus feet,” and I cannot rise up again until you grant me your mercy and correct my foolish ways.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is true that through your transcendental literatures you have given the world everything. But it is also true that in this same world there are those who have given up everything and taken upon their heads your order as their only possession. And it is these devotees only to whom you actually reveal everything. Your instructions have appeared in their hearts especially, as forceful as thunder and as clear as pure light, and they have burned to ashes all the sins of their former life /lives. These persons never want to deviate from your lotus feet because, simply, they are fixed in their faith, in their *sādhana,* in their chastity, and the proper use of their free will. I offer my obeisances unto them and always desire their company

Outside the shade of the instructions of one’s spiritual master, actual transcendental realization is not achieved. Outside of that shelter, what may pass as advancement is merely the bewildering shadow of spiritual understanding. What is gained by the ascending process is always proud of its own self-advancement and accumulation. But when one is actually blessed by the dust of the lotus feet of one’s bona fide spiritual master, or in other words when one is blessed because of pleasing him, then *siddhānta* is properly understood and attained.

*yasya deve parā bhaktir yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**“Only unto those great souls who have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of Vedic knowledge automatically revealed.” (*****Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad*** **6.23)**

By pleasing the spiritual master one comes to know and realize all esoteric truths on the progressive path of *bhakti.* The Lord Himself is the deliverer of the *jīva,* but He delivers him only when He sees the *jīva* serving His bona fide representative. You have often said that this is the secret of advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and it is confirmed both by your own perfect example and by śāstric evidence.

Thus one of your greatest glories and instructions to us is your chastity to the order of your spiritual master. Your strict practice of presenting the Absolute Truth as He is shows us how one should carefully avoid exhibiting ones own self-realization and advancement for the sake of false prestige, profit, and distinction. (One who is actually advanced sees himself as the least advanced and sees all others as serving the Lord better than he.) You hardly ever spoke of you own realization, yet that never gave us reason to doubt your authority in the *paramparā*. You are our greatest preceptor and our perfect example, and there is also no doubt whatsoever of your obvious self-realization, for you always spoke and wrote from the platform of realized knowledge (*vijñāna*). But despite all of this you still never touted your own personal realization to your disciples or your readers. Indeed, your deep emotions and spiritual experiences were almost always hidden away. This I find most intriguing as a truly perfect example of spiritual chastity.

There are many who are very eager and very quick to tell the world, or whoever will listen, of their understanding and advancement on the path of *bhakti*. I offer my respects to all such persons, but I am also, as you have ordered, wary of them. Your potency and preaching example for purifying the hearts of all conditioned souls proceeded only from your undeviating and relentless presentation of the absolute Vedic evidence and conclusions. Your personal realization, even your *svarūpa* (esoteric spiritual identity), is your own subjective possession and not meant for the inspection, understanding, or guidance of others. We do not try to impudently enter into that subject. In this world it is not our duty or our business.

True to your actual identity as Jagad Guru and our eternal spiritual master, you did not present to the world your own subjective truths and realizations (even though they are perfect also) as the absolute guidelines for all souls. But as a surrendered servant of Guru and Gaurāṅga, you humbly presented the topmost Vedic knowledge and its conclusions without adulteration or even, for that matter, subjective opinion. It is a wonderful example, and we have only to follow in your footsteps. You are truly chaste to the order and service of your spiritual master, and you are teaching us to be simply nothing more than the same. You have said many times that this mood is the cause of your own spiritual success in pleasing Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī. So the same method and mood must necessarily satisfy us. Then only can we remain just as you are—ever humble, even in the glory of practical transcendental realization, and we can continue to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness *as it is,* purely on your behalf and without the obscurities of our own infinitesimal spiritual subjectivities.

You have given Kṛṣṇa consciousness directly from the Absolute’s perspective, from Kṛṣṇa’s perspective, from Kṛṣṇa’s point of view. You have given His infinite personality, His absolute purpose, His reasons, and His full *akhila-rasāmṛta-mūrti*. And I am not talking of relative subjective perceptions, symbolic representations, empty ritualistic displays, or linguistic analogies. I speak of absolute realities, which are directly perceived, but only through the transparent via medium of a bona fide spiritual master coming in disciplic succession.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have factually delivered the inconceivable to those who are unworthy and unable to conceive it, simply by the humble and pure method of perfect representation. This is your glory and your true expertise, and I say most emphatically to everyone that there is no greater mercy received, no higher service rendered, and nothing more worthy of achievement than what is found confidentially hidden “deep in the dust of your lotus feet.”

Your servants at New Govardhana-dhāma, Murwillumbah, Australia.

## New Govardhana (USA)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Every day the devotees sing *śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu, adhama janāra bandhu:* “The spiritual master is an ocean of mercy and the friend of all the fallen souls.” We can’t imagine souls more fallen than we. Anyone who even quickly glances at our culture can see that we come from the land of the fallen. We have covered the land with concrete and asphalt, with housing developments and shopping malls (our culture’s version of places of pilgrimage). We slaughter animals and trees, exploit and abuse each other in countless, unspeakable ways. We do all this with progressive enthusiasm in pursuit of sense pleasure. All our endeavors simply entangle us more firmly in the grip of illusion and increase our suffering. Still, we remain so attached to that misery that, like Plato’s cave dwellers, we mock and threaten anyone who suggests a better life.

Nevertheless, you come, unbidden by us, but at your master’s behest, to tell us of life beyond the cave. While we delight in the shadow-puppet shows, identifying with first this shadow, then that, celebrating the quality of the links which bind us, you coax us, try to convince us that this show is not real life, this cave not our home, that you can lead us, if only we’ll let you, to the real world, our real home, the transcendental realm of Goloka.

Only a few take any notice; most of us, certain the cave is all there is, dismiss you as foolish, mad, or deceitful. We who try to follow you, though, find it hard, accustomed as we are to our bondage. We tend to hesitate, look back, resume our seats, sometimes even try to refasten our shackles, although we know better. Undismayed, you take our hands, cajole us, and promise that this is no bluff. Trusting you, we struggle toward the light at the cave’s entrance.

Like the ocean, your mercy knows no bounds, often all but forcing us to forsake our bondage. As Plato’s cave-dwellers had to relinquish all consideration of the relative merits of cave life’s various features, so you have admonished us that, by engaging our senses in devotional activity, we may also give up our attachments to all this world promises. You have often repeated the instruction of *Nārada Pañcarātra*:

*sarvopādhi-vinirmuktaṁ tat-paratvena nirmalam*  
*hṛṣīkeṇa hṛṣīkeśa-sevanaṁ bhaktir ucyate*

**When we free ourselves from all material designations and purify our senses by the** ***bhakti*** **process, we can understand the transcendental world. Only then can we be free.**

We see practical signs of your mercy: you came and lived among us, showed us how we could gradually understand spiritual life, showed us how we could gradually prepare for our return to the spiritual realm, and exhorted us to try, by any means at our disposal, to convince others to join us. The Vaiṣṇavas, you taught us, are *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī:* they cannot tolerate seeing others suffer due to ignorance. As Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu taught us, we should engage all our assets in work that benefits others:

*prāṇinām upakārāya yad eveha paratra ca*  
*karmaṇā manasā vācā tad eva mati-mān bhajet*

**Therefore, as we struggle to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in this corner of North America, we hope that we may always remain faithful to your aspiration for us: that, following in the footsteps of the Lord’s transcendental devotees, we strive to establish a cooperative society with God at the center.**

Because you came, many are losing faith in cave life and accepting you as the guide to the ultimate challenge—going back home, back to Godhead. We pray that, as fallen as we are, we may derive from your example the strength to keep our eyes turned toward the light of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the purity to emulate your tolerance and mercy.

Your servants at New Govardhana, San Diego, California.  
(written by Babhru dāsa)

## New Jersey

My dear eternal father,

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have picked us up from the hellish mentality of enjoying this temporary material world, forgetting our real self. You have taught us the real message of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in a very practical, simple, and sublime way. Anyone who takes this path is surely on the way back to Godhead, where there is eternity, knowledge, and bliss, for which we are hankering from time immemorial. Even the most sinful and iron-hearted man was affected by you because of your touchstone qualities of a pure devotee of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Without you, we had no aim in our life. We were wandering without the captain of the ship. You have mercifully and very tactfully given us our real identity, our relationship with the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, our goal of life, and the means to achieve that goal. For the Kali-yuga-dwelling envious, proud man you have given the perfect message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and the six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana.

You are specially empowered by Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and He chose you as His special messenger to preach His message all over the world. You are His instrument to fight the intense *māyā* of this Kali-yuga. His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura also predicted your appearance and hankered for it so that the dense darkness of this Kali-yuga would be dissipated. You are a *mahā-mahā-bhāgavata*. History has never seen a personality like you or close to you, and the world will never see your like again.

Your qualities are unlimited and full of transcendental bliss. A tiny fallen soul like me cannot touch even a glimpse of them or describe them properly. You are so compassionate and magnanimous that you came to the Western world at such an advanced age, created this wonderful International Society for Krishna Consciousness for our enlivening association, and gave us beautiful Deities like Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa, the all-auspicious holy name of Kṛṣṇa, and delicious *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us yourself without asking for anything in return. You are the real *patita-pāvana,* and you have dedicated your every breath to spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Although I am the lowest of men and have no good qualification of any kind, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have mercifully bestowed upon me some attraction to devotees, yourself, and Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Kindly remove all my *anarthas* of *kāma, krodha, lobha, moha, mada,* and *mātsarya,* as well as attraction for this temporary, miserable material world, including the subtle allurements of profit, adoration, and distinction (*lābha, pūjā, pratiṣṭhā*), so that I will become attached to your ever-soothing lotus feet forever and develop eagerness to serve Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and Their devotees.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at ISKCON of New Jersey, USA.  
(written by Śamīka Ṛṣi dāsa)

## New Jhārikhaṇḍa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Prayer in Separation**

Bathing so deep in your love,  
Stopping, remembering the moments, every moment  
Of each time, how it was with you.  
Stopping, letting in the remembered feeling of eternal love,  
The feeling of transcending and  
How the worldly cares would pass by  
And nothing really mattered, resting in the most serene peace.  
Your words  
Protect the memory of your faith, hold these undying thoughts of you.  
Allow me to enter always into your words of truth,  
Allow me to come there again and again and hear and feel  
Your presence, your words.  
Do not leave me in this foreign land without you.  
And in the end of it all, entrust me to your keeping  
Even though I am useless.  
I entered this world unknowingly, you have given this life meaning.  
Let me die remembering you.  
Take me where you are.  
Ease this pain of separation.

The pain is easing, you are with me.  
The world is fading away, and now we are again together.  
I feel safe in your words, your presence is peace.  
Help me stay with you.  
The path is difficult and I am weak, with no special worth.  
I am afraid and yet unafraid as I feel you near.  
Your servant Bīr Mālā dāsī prays, Please keep me with you.

Dearest master, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your most auspicious appearance!

O spiritual master of the worlds, you have entrusted us with your temple, one of the army bases for fighting the material energy. Please give us intelligence to understand your strategies for us, and the spiritual strength to execute them without falling. Please grant us the purity to bring in souls from the other side, and endow us with the love to keep those souls in your lines. Teach us the humility to see all other servants closer to you than we know, and compassion to care for the wounded ones. Bestow upon us enthusiasm to never stop working hard for you. Let us be committed to serving you, year after year, lifetime after lifetime. Tie us to you, so we may never leave your side.

You have given us life, and meaning to life. In return, we can offer you our souls, bodies, words, and minds. Let us use our time only for doing what you wanted us to do—worshiping Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva, performing *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* distributing *prasādam,* studying *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā,* delivering your books to others, caring for your devotees. We will do these for you for as long as you want. We are terribly fallen, so we pray for your utmost mercy.

Trying to serve you,  
*Your devotees at the New* Jhārikhaṇḍa *Mandir, Laguna Beach, California, USA.*

## New Naimiṣāraṇya

Very dear Śrīla Prabhupada,

We were born in the darkness of ignorance, but you opened our eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

This being the last year of this century, many people in the external world are glorifying their candidates for Person of the Century. Without a doubt, we all know that only one person could hold that place in our hearts:

His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, Saint of the Century!

You revealed the full potency of Lord Caitanya’s mission to deliver the fallen souls from this material world back home to the spiritual world. *Ekaś candras tamo hanti, na ca tāra sahasraśaḥ:* “One moon dissipates the darkness; thousands of stars cannot.” You are that one moon, Śrīla Prabhupada.

This also happens to be the last year of this millennium. After due deliberation, with great confidence, we also proclaim you to be:

His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, Personality of the Millennium!

Taking into consideration many other pure devotees who appeared earlier in the millennium (Śrīla Rūpa and Sanātana Gosvāmīs, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, to name a few), we feel that by honoring you we are also honoring the entire *paramparā.*

You told us, “Religion means direct touch with God. That is religion. So they have no idea of God. How there can be religion?” You broadcast the actual standard of religion: unalloyed devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You had the potency to transmit the Absolute Truth into the hearts of the conditioned souls. You taught us that there is no substitute for surrendering to Kṛṣṇa.

You, Śrīla Prabhupada, are transcendental, and one who associates with you immediately becomes situated above the modes of material nature, just as one who associates with an airplane immediately goes as fast as the plane. Attachment to ISKCON is not measured in formal membership or by wearing a *brāhmaṇa* thread, but in the purity with which we dedicate ourselves to your service. Our strength lies in how dependent we are on you.

Considering your astounding accomplishments, which affected the entire course of history in Kali-yuga, we unanimously bestow upon you the title of:

Śrīla Prabhupada, the Greatest Hero of Kali-yuga!

You told us, “ISKCON is my body, *saṅkīrtana* is my heart.” “Our books are keeping alive this movement.” “Book distribution solves all problems.” Please help us to distribute more of your books.

You liked Florida very much; you said the weather and the vegetation reminded you of India. Our main offering for you here in South Florida has taken us eight years to complete. It was a huge struggle, but with our president Keśava Mahārāja’s pushing and the cooperation of the entire congregation, it has become a success. It is a thirty-ton carved marble altar for the Deities and matching *vyāsāsana* for you. The same people who made your *samādhi* in Vṛndāvana and the Juhu temple carved the marble. Our goal is to expand and improve the standard of worship, with the understanding that everything else will become more auspicious if Lord Kṛṣṇa and His pure devotee are worshiped properly.

The result is stunning, Śrīla Prabhupada! It is the only carved marble *siṁhāsana* of its kind outside India, and even within India there are few like it. “Our presentation of Kṛṣṇa consciousness must always be very bold. If you are a king you must act like a king.”

We also established a *puṣpa-samādhi* for you in your new marble *vyāsāsana,* so that we may feel your presence even more intensely. Jewels, flower petals from your last garland, and pieces of a *dhotī* and *kurtā* that you wore were placed under your sitting place on the *vyāsāsana*. This was then consecrated, and your mercy and blessings were invoked with a four-*havana* fire *yajña.* Contact with your *samādhi*, which is *śuddha-sattva* (pure goodness) cleanses us of material impurities.

When asked if you were in the hearts of your disciples, you replied, “Yes, if you let me enter.” When asked how we could please you the most, you replied, “By loving Kṛṣṇa.” Our prayer is that you please enter our hearts and help us love Kṛṣṇa; please empower us more and more to serve you.

Your eternal servants at New Naimiṣāraṇya, Miami, Florida.  
(written by Nartaka Gopāla-devī dāsī)

## New Nandagram Varṇāśrama Village

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust at your lotus feet.

Your controlled mind ceaselessly looking for opportunities to encourage *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*.

Your untiring patience in your dealings with those around you.

The murmur of Kṛṣṇa’s names always seen upon your lips in private moments.

The seemingly endless vehicles by which you traversed the planet, the doors of which always opened upon new arenas for your preaching.

The beauty of your face and the eloquence of your movements; eyes always trained upon you, gleaning so much pleasure from the pleasure in your smile.

Your ability to paraphrase Kṛṣṇa consciousness in purport after purport, as brilliant as the sun.

Your complete conviction persuading the hearts of fallen souls that there is hope, purpose, and destination.

Your practicality—Kṛṣṇa consciousness in practice.

Your example of being Kṛṣṇa conscious is something which all the devotees of ISKCON hold very dear to their hearts. You are so obviously pure and single-minded, you inspire us to go on.

Our most beautiful Lord and His most beautiful associates given by you to us in perfect charity.

This is the meaning of our lives, so long laid waste.

Please help us by your mercy to show people how to live a simple life, free of anxiety for material needs, and full of the pleasure that is Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants at the New Nandagram Varṇāśrama Village, Bambra, Australia.

## New Navadvīpa (USA)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Here in Hawaii we are blessed with two acres of land that you yourself walked on many times, and that are graced by the beautiful banyan tree and Tulasī gardens you loved so much.

For your pleasure we are striving to carry on Deity worship very nicely. We have a Food for Life program distributing nice *prasādam* three times a week, *harināma-saṅkīrtana* every Saturday night in Waikiki, and continuous book distribution. Gauranga’s Restaurant is open six days a week for lunch and dinner, and there is, of course, the highlight of the week: The Sunday Feast.

Somehow or other, by your mercy, we are trying to be chaste to you and follow your instructions by carrying on these programs so dear to your heart. We hope that on this auspicious day of your appearance you will give us the strength and determination necessary to continue to serve you by taking care of the institution you established here in Hawaii so nicely.

We hope we can please you and become good instruments of Your Divine Grace so that we can give this Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others, because by doing so we can also please the whole disciplic succession and ultimately Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. Thank you very much for coming into this world and spreading this divine knowledge, especially to those of us here in the Western countries. We hope that by striving to become more humble than a blade of grass and more tolerant than a tree, we can ultimately help others to try to become your instruments as well.

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants at New Navadvīpa Dhāma, Honolulu, Hawaii.  
(written by Acintya dāsa)

## New Navadvīpa (Khasadeśa)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Your Divine Grace! And all glories to your merciful appearance!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you must be a very dear devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, because only one who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa and who is empowered by Him can spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

It is said in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.3.43) that “This *Bhāgavata Purāṇa* is as brilliant as the sun, and it has arisen just after the departure of Lord Kṛṣṇa to His own abode, accompanied by religion, knowledge, etc. Persons who have lost their vision due to the dense darkness of ignorance in the Age of Kali shall get light from this *Purāṇa*.” In the present situation in Khasadeśa we are really living in the darkest ignorance. We are born in darkness, having no knowledge. We are controlled by the three modes of material nature. We indulge in false freedom without knowing what is our real interest or who is our real friend and master. We indulge in this false existence thinking that “I’m this body,” and in this way we gradually meet our death.

The light of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* which can save the whole humanity, was locked in the ancient Sanskrit language. It was you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who kindly unlocked it, and thus the light of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* can now shine upon the whole world.
